country_event = {
	id = 7000
	title = "EVTNAME7000"
	desc = "EVTDESC7000"
	picture = "Administration"
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = primary_culture_must_be_changed
		}
		NOT = {
			any_owned_province = {
				any_pop = {
					is_primary_culture = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 9
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA7000"
		set_country_flag = primary_culture_must_be_changed
	}
}

	}
}
